6. Proposed roster roles and role coverage
Roles should be tags, not mutually exclusive classes. One shinobi may be a bruiser + counterattacker; another
may be a healer + chakra battery. Role tags are useful to the team-builder, but a unit’s skill interactions—not
its label—determine its actual performance.

10.1 One worked example: synergy versus raw power
Illustrative encounter: the opponent fields a high-stat carry, a healer, and three damage units with no cleanse
or protection. Our lower-power team uses a mark setter, controller, protector, finisher, and healer. On turn 1
the controller slows the enemy carry; the mark setter marks the healer; the protector absorbs a flank attack;
on turn 2 the finisher gains bonus damage against the marked healer and removes the sustain source. The
opponent has higher raw stats, but its win engine is disrupted. The result is not guaranteed: if the mark is
resisted, the controller is interrupted, or the opponent has an accessible cleanser, our setup may fail. That
uncertainty is the point—synergy creates a plan, not an automatic victory.




            Scope research • Hero Roster v1.10 • Historical Sections 6, 10, 26, 69 preserved • Page 4

26. Starter roster-to-archetype matrix and combat closure gate
(Version 0.8 baseline; clarified in 0.9)
Purpose. This chapter fills the major design gap that remained after the combat rules were written: we had
defined roles and 10+ archetypes, but had not yet mapped them into a coherent, replaceable starter roster.
The roster below is made of functional placeholders, not final licensed Naruto kits. Canon names, likenesses,
story elements and trademark use require appropriate rights. The important deliverable is the role-and-
counter architecture; characters and animations can be mapped later.

Fairness requirement. The first copy of every essential function must work. Duplicates, rare variants and
premium skins may offer customization, alternate conditions or modest efficiency, but must not unlock a
character’s only viable skill rotation, required counter, or the only route to a tactical archetype. No single
placeholder is mandatory: every roster function has a substitute pathway.

26.1 A character kit contract: fields every future shinobi must specify
                                                                                                           Why it matters for team-building / F2P
 Field                                                Required definition
                                                                                                           fairness
 Role identity                                        One primary role and no more than two                Prevents a hybrid from being best-in-slot as
                                                      secondary roles; name the explicit                   damage dealer, protector and controller at
                                                      opportunity cost of each secondary function.         once.
 Basic / Technique / Ultimate                         State target legality, exact effects, damage         Every hero can function at baseline copy;
                                                      bucket, duration timing, Chakra gain,                effects cannot hide triggers or create recursive
                                                      cooldown and whether it is a normal action or        actions.
                                                      a bounded follow-up.
 Auto decision                                        One visible condition for Technique and a            The idle promise stays complete and players
                                                      deterministic Basic fallback; Ultimate priority      do not buy a better combat AI.
                                                      is explicit.
 Setup and conversion                                 Write “This kit sets up X; it converts X into Y”;    A skill’s synergy is testable rather than just a
                                                      if it does not need setup, name its reliable         vague description.
                                                      fallback.
 Weakness and counter                                 Name at least two distinct counter categories        Stops one dominant kit becoming an
                                                      that can challenge its strongest line.               unanswerable gacha gate.
 Replacement                                          Name at least one accessible unit that can           The team archetype remains buildable
                                                      perform the same function with a different           without one named hero or duplicate.
                                                      trade-off.
 Investment scaling                                   Separate what level/gear change from what            Avoids investing into a hero’s basic identity

26.2 Nineteen placeholder kits for the first coherent roster
Numbers in this roster are either references to existing authoritative prototype rules (for example Mark +10%,
Wound 18% source ATK, personal Chakra) or deliberately qualitative kit definitions. Do not treat unspecified
skill coefficients as final values. The role shells are intended to prove roster coverage, not a final hero tier list.

26.3 Ten coherent five-shinobi archetypes and their tactical loops
These are team shells, not mandatory five-character recipes. K-IDs refer to the placeholder kits in Section 26.2.
A functioning roster should offer more than one viable unit for each essential function as it grows; the starter
roster must include at least one base-copy provider for each required answer category.

different functional categories. A new unit cannot be released as the only answer to an existing strategy; any
replacement must be accessible through starter, standard acquisition or a deterministic earnable route.

26.5 Synergy-strength budget and substitution test
      Use a visible setup → conversion → opponent response chain. A combo is meaningful if it improves time-
       to-knockout, damage dealt/prevented, survival or action economy—not merely if a tooltip displays +stats.
      Measure a synergy against a functionally equivalent substitute at equal investment. The initial acceptance
       target is that removing one named partner should not create more than an 8 percentage-point win-rate
       loss when an accessible substitute restores the same role. This is a test threshold, not a universal industry
       metric.
      Village/faction bonuses share one total cap: 2 matching tags = +2% ATK/HP, 3 = +4%, 4–5 = +5% maximum.
       Use only the highest reached tier; do not add tier rewards together. Relationship effects should usually
       add utility or a one-time small follow-up, with an average normalized effect swing targeted at 3% or less.
      The core kit expresses its role at baseline copy. Duplicate upgrades may improve numbers modestly
       within shared caps, or grant non-combat customization; they cannot unlock a missing cleanse, an extra
       mandatory action, the only anti-shield effect, or the only source of a core archetype trigger.
      No automatic global elemental rock-paper-scissors multiplier in MVP. Element/nature tags may affect a
       specific previewed skill or boss condition, but do not add a universal hidden damage multiplier until all
       sides of the matrix have accessible answers.
      A hybrid unit must pay an opportunity cost. A healer-buffer that also deals carry-level burst at full uptime
       is not “flexible”; it erases the reason to choose specialists. Balance that with lower damage, a conditional
       effect, cooldown trade-off, limited target range or reduced protection uptime.

26.6 One worked damage / synergy example (illustrative, not a balance claim)
Assume an accessible Mark setter applies +10% Vulnerability, an accessible DEF debuffer reduces DEF by 20%,
and a finisher hits a target with ATK=1,200, coefficient=1.6 and no crit. Target DEF starts at 1,000. Using
Section 24.6, DEF multiplier is 1000/(1000+1000)=0.5 before setup. With 20% DEF reduction, Effective DEF is
800 and multiplier is 1000/1800≈0.5556. If the finisher also has a +20% Attack Bonus, the estimated hit is
floor(1200×1.6×1.20×0.5556×1.10)≈1,408 damage. If a raw-stat alternative has 10% more ATK (1,320) but no

                      Scope research • Hero Roster v1.10 • Historical Sections 6, 10, 26, 69 preserved • Page 9
<PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 10 / 148>
                                 NARUTO IDLE RPG | SCOPE MASTER 03 | HERO ROSTER v1.10
Mark, no DEF reduction and no Attack Bonus, its matching hit is floor(1320×1.6×0.5)=1,056. Under these
assumptions, the prepared combination hits harder despite the alternative’s higher raw ATK.

This demonstrates why composition can matter; it does not establish that the prepared team is always
superior. The setup sequence can fail if Mark is cleansed, the debuffer is interrupted or the finisher is
intercepted. Those response windows are essential. Compare the whole team over repeated matchups, not one
damage number, and measure what happens when one enabler is removed.

This checklist is part of the implementation contract. A character cannot introduce a private exception to a
core combat rule. New exceptions require a written design reason, a logged decision, counterplay review, and
an automated regression test.

26.8 Combat written-specification closure and future implementation gate
The public-source comparison now covers the major team-combat patterns relevant to this design:
faction/formation bonuses (AFK Arena/Journey and Mythic Heroes), role coverage and roster transfer
convenience (Mobile Legends: Adventure), large teams and faction aura (Idle Heroes), hard action sequences
(NIKKE), Speed/Combat Readiness systems (Epic Seven, Summoners War, RAID), shared action
                 Scope research • Hero Roster v1.10 • Historical Sections 6, 10, 26, 69 preserved • Page 10
<PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 11 / 148>
                          NARUTO IDLE RPG | SCOPE MASTER 03 | HERO ROSTER v1.10
resources/weakness break (Honkai: Star Rail), turn-based positioning (Brown Dust 2), card/action resources
(Reverse: 1999), and tag/role/previewed-weakness team rules (Cookie Run: Kingdom). Sources have different
authority levels, so this is a pattern audit—not an exhaustive account-level or hidden-formula audit of every
game.

The combat source-of-truth checklist is a continuing specification-quality requirement, not a gate that blocks
broader conceptual research. Undefined combat rules must remain visible in the open-decision register, and
future implementation or empirical balance claims remain gated by Section 24.15.

69. Naruto-canon hero theory: five candidates per active
combat role
8 November 2026 | Live master v0.10.42 | Owner-directed scope pivot. Priority 8B account/backup research is
PAUSED, not completed. This is a Naruto-canon character and five-shinobi team-composition theory pass only.
No playable implementation, simulator, balance certification, artwork, or commercial license is implied.

69.1 Scope, source of truth, and why the number is 65
[PROJECT DIRECTION] The owner requested five recognizable Naruto-verse heroes for each existing game
category. Section 24/§26 and the thirteen core role profiles in the role-budget table remain authoritative.
Primary roles are tags for sorting, not exclusive classes: each of the 65 distinct heroes receives ONE primary
placement, but can have secondary synergy tags. Thirteen groups × five characters = 65 distinct canon-
character concepts; no character is duplicated across primary groups. This is the FIRST DESIGN BATCH, not a
promise that all 65 will ship on day one.

[SCOPE RULE] The earlier broad tag table also mentions Reviver, but later core balancing deliberately
excluded revival from the first roster. Accordingly it is NOT a fourteenth active group. Chiyo can heal/protect
without an automatic resurrection. If the owner later elects to add revive, treat that as a separate combat-
engine redesign and balance gate.

[CANON / GAME MECHANICS BOUNDARY] Character identity and signature fiction are sourced from Naruto
and Naruto Shippuden. Techniques, status percentages, healing suppression, shield values, Chakra gifts, target
rules and reaction triggers are design translations, NOT claims that the Naruto canon literally defines these
game effects. Example: Sakura demonstrably treats Sasori poison; one-source generic Wound cleansing is our
mechanic. Shino insects canonically drain Chakra; their proposed Healing Suppression is a deliberate game
adaptation. Sasori is a stronger canon precedent for poison-based healing suppression.

[TIMELINE / IP] The roster intentionally mixes people from different eras and factions. A cross-era
Chronicle/Archive what-if framing would be required; it does not mean all were allies or alive together in the
original story. Naruto names, visuals, dialogue and likenesses are research references and require proper
rights for a commercial game. The existing original-IP alternative remains preserved.

[COMBAT CONTRACT] Five unique deployed heroes, two front and three rear. Personal Chakra: Basic +25;
Technique +15; Ultimate costs 100; one normal action per living unit per round; two subsequent own-turn
cooldown after Technique; one Ultimate condition at a time; bounded follow-ups and summons; shared status
stacking and hard-control caps. No hero-specific power may silently override §§24–27, §49 or normalized
Arena rules.

[PLAYER ACCESS] START00 grants Gaara/Sasuke/Neji/Naruto. START01 grants the owner exactly one explicit
choice: Sakura OR Shizune. STORY01 grants Shikamaru, STORY02 grants Temari before the Decoy stage,
STORY03 grants Shino before the enemy-healing gate. Those source IDs and reward quantities are unchanged;
we merely attach proposed canon identities. The deterministic story-source strategy, not banner luck, must
provide the functional answers.

[DESIGN CONTRADICTION TO RESOLVE LATER] These four starter characters come from different canonical
teams/factions, and several enemy-era villains are candidate heroes. The original Broken Supply Route story
must be framed as a noncanonical Chronicle/Tactical Archive encounter or carefully rewritten; do NOT claim
this is canonical Naruto plot chronology.

69.7 Prioritization, rarity and next design handoff
[PHASE 1 — PAPER LOCK] 65 distinct canon identity concepts across 13 groups. The immediately actionable
nine start/story kits above inherit existing K-ID contracts. The other ten K-ID identities map to already-defined
shells but still need their unspecifed first-copy skills or Ultimates completed. The remaining 46 heroes are
future character-design candidates within this proposed batch, not automatically configured launch rewards
or paid exclusives.

[RARITY] Canonical strength is NOT a direct stat ranking. Naruto, Sasuke, Gaara and Neji can be free core
characters in early forms. Later “Sage Mode Naruto” or “Susanoo Sasuke” variants would require distinct,
documented kit opportunity costs; an alternate canon form must not silently make a free starter obsolete or
become the sole source of an essential counter. Do not assign pulls, star tiers, banners, team power or release
dates yet.

[RELEASE-SCOPE DECISION OPEN] The owner asked for five per category as the INITIAL HERO THEORY
BATCH. This creates a 65-hero design pool, not a 65-character balanced launch commitment. Before release
scoping, compare (A) mapping 19 K-ID placeholders to canon, (B) a viable 25–35-character pool, and (C) the


            Scope research • Hero Roster v1.10 • Historical Sections 6, 10, 26, 69 preserved • Page 22
<PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 23 / 148>
                      NARUTO IDLE RPG | SCOPE MASTER 03 | HERO ROSTER v1.10
full 65-character launch. Measure animation cost, balance combinatorics, accessible counters and future
release cadence. The owner may still elect to introduce additional heroes later.

[REJECTED] No arbitrary 14th Reviver group; no shared magical “chakra=mana” invented powers without
canon inspiration; no duplicate primary placements; no tier list based on lore power alone; no Chiyo
resurrection at first copy; no canon-clashing story claims; no exclusive support making one specific team
compulsory.

[HOLD] Priority 8B technical-account study remains PAUSED at the owner’s request. Roster theory is now the
active focus. Next recommended research: EXTRA HIGH for Priority H2 “Nine first-copy canon kit contracts
and 19-shell reconciliation,” because each new identity must be matched to existing Chakra, damage,
cooldown, status, combat target and acquisition rules. Do not proceed into production or live testing without
explicit owner authorization.

70. H2 — Nine first-copy Naruto hero kits and five-slot team
coherence
8 November 2026 | Scope Master 03 v1.1 | RESEARCH-ONLY AUTHORING PACKET. This chapter adds
design detail; it does not replace historical Section 69, change the combat engine, alter
START00/START01/STORY receipts, or authorize implementation.

[STATUS KEY] REUSED = existing rule carried forward without changing value; SPECIFIED = new explicit
wording or player-facing presentation; PROPOSED = reversible hypothesis requiring later review; BLOCKED =
would require a separate combat/encounter revision. Canon names and jutsu are fictional-source inspiration;
precise RPG coefficients and effect tags are invented game rules.

70.1 Canon/version boundary, source hierarchy and invariants
The nine first-copy heroes are Gaara (K-01), Sasuke Uchiha (K-03), Neji Hyuga (K-12), Naruto Uzumaki (K-19),
Sakura Haruno (K-09), Shizune (K-10), Shikamaru Nara (K-08), Temari (K-04) and Shino Aburame (K-17). Early
recognizable forms are the visual reference. Later powered-up versions are separate candidate kits, not
hidden upgrades or new skills retroactively added to these base copies.

[AUTHORITY] (1) Scope 02 / original Sections 24–27 establishes universal combat mechanics; (2) Scope 08 /
Sections 45, 48, 49 and 51 establishes the exact nine opening Techniques, Ultimates and relevant Hall20
reference stats; (3) this Section 70 defines Naruto terminology, nonnumerical passive presentation,
unambiguous first-copy UI/AI documentation, and team theory. If numbers conflict, Scope 08 wins until
explicitly repriced.

70.3 Nine hero passports: canon motif, passive, actions and counterplay
[PASSIVE DECISION] The passives below deliberately have informational/target-legibility value and NO
additional numeric combat effect. This protects every previously authored Hall20 G1–G8 certificate from a
silent buff. A later numeric-passive pass is allowed, but it must be tested and recorded separately in Scope 02
and Scope 08 before becoming active.

normal actions; stored 100 ineligible-ultimate Chakra does not disappear.

[OPEN — REAL COMBAT PASSES] The first nine hero cards have explicit named Basics, informative passives,
inherited Techniques and Ultimates, targeting/AI, visible counterplay and five-slot compositions. They are
internally bounded written designs, NOT proven gameplay balance or complete content. Real active passives,
persistent late-game stat curves, visual/audio effects, relationship synergies, 100-Chakra late-match play and
the other ten K-shell Ultimates remain unsolved.

[OPEN — TEAM COMP FAIRNESS] After the nine, author the remaining ten K-ID first-copy contracts rather
than jumping directly to production of all 65 identities. Later, compare each of the 13 × five role pools against
an attainable substitute, with no mandatory canon pair or premium counter. Never infer a rarity tier from
lore strength alone.

[NEXT RECOMMENDED LEVEL: EXTRA HIGH] H3: quantify and challenge nine passives against existing eight-
gate ledgers and design the remaining ten K-shell First-Copy Ultimates without reopening the entire economy.
If active passives cause any coefficients, target access, status applications, action count or Chakra changes,
update Scope 02 and Scope 08 together before calling the kit coherent.

[CURRENT RESEARCH PHASE] Only Master 03 is edited in this packet. Priority 8B remains paused. No game
code, art assets, animated attacks, executable simulations, or live tests have been produced.

71. H3A — Three main roles, thirteen preserved specializations
and passive-budget control
[RESEARCH STATUS — 8 November 2026] User-approved classification simplification; authoring-only scope
change. This adds three player-facing main roles without removing, merging, or rewriting any of the 13
historical specialization categories or their 65 Naruto character candidates. Existing K-IDs, stats, skills, five-
person formation, acquisition gates and archived encounter arithmetic are unchanged.

71.1 Problem, proposed solution and distinction between role and specialty
The existing 13 role categories accurately describe combat functions but are too many to expose as 13 equally
prominent hero classes. The new UI presents exactly THREE broad main roles: TANK (absorb or redirect
damage), DAMAGE DEALER (defeat enemies through burst or attrition), and SUPPORT (enable allies or disrupt
enemies). The existing 13 labels become SPECIALIZATION TAGS; they continue to define targeting,
counterplay and kit intent.

[IMPORTANT] Roles classify a hero’s PRIMARY JOB in a specific kit, not the character’s entire canon identity.
Each hero has exactly one visible primary umbrella role, one principal historical specialization tag, and at
most two additional functional tags for team-building. Subtags may be shared across main roles. The primary
role does NOT enforce a particular formation slot, nor does it apply a hidden stat modifier.

Role, specialization, tactical effect and canon affiliation are separate dimensions. For example: Gaara — Tank /
Guardian / Taunt; Sakura — Support / Healer–Cleanser / removes one Wound; Naruto — Damage Dealer /
Hybrid–Flex / reliable striker. These terms should not automatically change combat behavior.
 Primary role                    Player question              Typical functions              What it does NOT imply
                                                              Guard, Taunt, body-block,      No universal rear-row
 Tank                            Who protects the squad?      temporary shields, durable     immunity; no compulsory
                                                              off-tanking                    two-Tank quota
                                                              Finisher, AoE, sustained
                                                                                             No automatic highest ATK or
 Damage Dealer                   Who defeats enemy units?     damage, rear access,
                                                                                             unconditional one-shot
                                                              offensive summons
                                                              Heal, cleanse, Mark, debuff,   Not synonymous with
                                 Who enables the team or
 Support                                                      buff, interrupt, control,      healing; a Support can deal
                                 restricts enemies?
                                                              Chakra aid                     damage

Hybrid / Flex remains the thirteenth specialization and is not promoted into a fourth umbrella class.
Summoner / Clone and Counterattacker / Trapper also remain full specialties rather than unexplained
“miscellaneous” classes. Their primary role is resolved per hero by the primary value they provide in five-
shinobi combat.

specialties. The per-hero primary role below is a NEW DISPLAY PROPOSAL, not a canon power ranking or
balance statement. The original roster tables, character names, and prototype techniques remain verbatim
earlier in this file.

[PLAYER CHOICE] Starter A and B both provide one Tank (Gaara), two Damage Dealers (Sasuke and Naruto),

fielded shinobi and never create a sixth active combatant.

impose mandatory quotas or place all Tanks in front automatically.

[VALIDATION TEST] Use exactly five DIFFERENT heroes in two frontline and three rear slots. Primary role

minimums without a separately reviewed combat change. Existing 10 archetypes (§26.3 and §69.5) are not
deleted or narrowed by the five illustrative teams above.

71.7 H3 active-passive budget audit: keep nine prior encounter certificates intact
Research verdict: zero active numerical passives are approved in this classification pass. All nine H2
informational passives stay in force. There is no legitimate “free” passive budget merely because a hero was
assigned a simpler role badge; any new combat effect changes at least one damage, timing, survival, targeting
or Chakra dependency.

72. H3B — Character-building closure: 19 core K-ID contracts
and 46 future first-copy kits
Date: 8 November 2026 | Master 03 v1.3 | Status: complete paper authorship, NOT validated combat balance.
Sources of authority: Scope 02 §§24–27; Scope 08 §§43–56; Master 03 §§26, 69–71. Technical work 8B remains
paused. No implementation or executable combat simulations.

Definition of “finished for now”: 65 individually named characters retain one primary role, an exact Hall-20
reference stat proposal, Basic, Passive, Technique, Ultimate, AI conditions, two counters, and release
classification. The nine canonical H2 starter/story cards are frozen word-for-word. Newly written standard-
pool and future kits remain design proposals and MUST NOT be described as empirically balanced or launch-
approved.

73. H3B — Paper balance audit and active-passive control
This is a paper-only algebraic/invariant audit. It does not represent simulated win rates, real outcomes, AI
behavior in an executable engine or empirical fairness. Existing combat reference remains §27; nine
starter/story skills remain frozen as §70.

Decision H3B-P: P0 informational/AI passives are canonical for this paper version. P1 numeric active passives
are recorded as candidates only. To approve a P1: revise action/skill budgets in Scope 02, recalculate all
affected full five-person G1–G8 ledgers in Scope 08, and inspect normalized-Arena (Scope 10) cross-roster
effects.

Risk warning: the extreme unconditioned 1.50× finisher calculation leaves only 103 HP (about 2.0% of a 5,100-
HP finisher) under the listed maximum buff/critical/DEF reduction assumptions. This is a narrow analytical
margin, NOT a robust all-opponents one-shot guarantee. One minor passive, stat-growth rule or changed
target DEF could invalidate it. No extra damage passives approved until this margin is revisited.

Further risk: 46 future concepts use shared coefficient/condition families for initial parity. Their lore names
and intended counters differ, but several could feel mechanically repetitive in practice. Individuality and 65-
by-65 directed matchups are NOT certified. Multiple decoy-creators in one team may saturate single-target
attacks even though each decoy is individually capped; this must be audited before any such lineup is
released.

75. H4 — Hero balance, mechanical diversity and directed
matchup risk
Research date: 9 November 2026. Scope: Master 03 only. Authority: unchanged combat §27 (Scope 02), first
eight campaign ledgers (Scope 08), and hero contracts §§69–74 (this Master). This is a static paper audit; no
executable battle, seeded win-rate test or actual player study was performed.

Decision H4-0: preserve all 65 canon hero identities and the 3 main roles/13 specialization groups. Freeze the
19 provisional K-ID contracts for the existing encounter theory. Treat any changes to the 46 future-release kits
as NOT APPROVED suggestions until they are rewritten as complete legal contracts and cross-scope audited.

75.1 Static design-method audit and honest limits
We extracted all 56 newly authored §72.4–72.5 first-copy cards: ten core K-ID cards and 46 planned-release
cards. For each card, remove the fictional attack name before the em dash and compare the remaining
Technique and Ultimate effect wording as an ordered pair. This is stricter than similarity by class name, but
deliberately narrower than identity across all stats, AI and counter text.
 Metric                                    Exact audit result                               Interpretation

                                                                                            Nine earlier starters in §70 were frozen
 New §72.4–72.5 cards                      56 (10 core + 46 future)
                                                                                            outside this comparison.
                                                                                            Many characters reuse the same core
 Unique Technique/Ultimate effect pairs    22
                                                                                            tactical package.
                                                                                            Means duplicated effect wording, NOT
 Cards in a shared effect-pair cluster     50 of 56
                                                                                            50 totally identical heroes.
                                                                                            High-priority differentiation backlog; 3

                                                                                            future cards are unique by this test.
                                                                                            They share templates with future cards;

                                                                                            No identity, rarity or availability

                                                                                            deleted.
Disposition: this finding corrects any impression that all 65 existing cards have distinct strategic identities.
The §72 first-copy authorship milestone stands, but mechanical differentiation is OPEN for most planned-
release heroes. The exact collision fingerprints and the 56 source descriptions are saved in the accompanying
H4 machine-readable audit.

Threat model: multiple same-purpose kits reduce roster-choice meaning; a higher-rarity copy can dominate
through nonmechanical advantages. However, similarity can be healthy when a functional alternative is
needed for free-player accessibility. The goal is meaningful differentiated trade-offs, not 65 unrelated
gimmicks.

75.4 Finisher upper-bound sensitivity and active-passive budget
Illustrative maximum-buff paper stress: ATK 1,250; +40% Attack Bonus; +25% Skill Bonus; +15% Vulnerability;
2.0× Crit; target DEF 850 with 40% capped reduction; DEF multiplier 1000/(1000+510). This is an extreme
conjunction of permitted caps, not a normal battle frequency estimate.
 Unconditional coefficient        Floored damage            5,100-HP remainder                Reference-only result

 1.50×                            4997                      103                               survives
 1.55×                            5164                      -64                               full-HP KO possible
 1.60×                            5331                      -231                              full-HP KO possible


              Scope research • Hero Roster v1.10 • Historical Sections 6, 10, 26, 69 preserved • Page 87
<PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 88 / 148>
                             NARUTO IDLE RPG | SCOPE MASTER 03 | HERO ROSTER v1.10
Even a +0.05× ATK change in an unconditioned attack crosses the 5,100-HP boundary in this specific reference.
This falsifies a naive “small passive = safe” assumption. It does not establish global one-shot safety against
every possible lower-HP target. Recommended hold: never activate unconditional direct-damage P1 passives
on the existing finisher group without rebuilding the full extreme-stack envelope and G1–G8 ledgers.
 Passive choice                            Paper decision                        Reason

                                                                                 Supports legibility and lore identity
 P0 informational/AI cue                   KEEP
                                                                                 without changing old combat outputs.
                                                                                 May cross one-shot or status thresholds
 P1 extra damage/stat buff                 DO NOT ACTIVATE
                                                                                 and force a silent reprice of encounters.
                                                                                 Must trade away a priced portion of an
 P1 zero-sum replacement                   FUTURE CANDIDATE                      existing Technique/Ultimate; compare
                                                                                 per-action and 3-round throughput.
                                                                                 Would pressure canon-only squads and
 Hero bonds and faction flat buffs         NO NUMERICAL BONUS APPROVED
                                                                                 undermine accessible substitutes.

76. H5 — Future-hero differentiation pilot and team-
counterplay bounds
Research date: 9 November 2026. Owner: Scope 03 (Hero Roster). Inputs: existing Scope 03 v1.4 §72–75, frozen
Scope 02 combat rules, frozen Scope 08 G1–G8 encounter paper certificates. All sixteen H5 variants are NEW
FUTURE-RELEASE PROPOSALS; they do not replace the earlier §72 cards, K-01–K-19 contracts, or official
combat mechanics.

Scope discipline: no new active numerical passives, new status category, additional normal action, paid role
monopoly, guaranteed conditional status or retroactive stage rebalance. Gameplay effects and Jutsu names
are adaptations, not canon numerical facts. All planned characters require appropriate rights for commercial
use.

76.1 Why this pilot and what it does not certify
The earlier exact-wording H4 static audit counted 22 distinct Technique+Ultimate effect pairs in 56 newly
authored cards and 50 heroes participating in a duplicated pair. H5 pilots sixteen high-overlap future cards
with explicit opportunity costs. We deliberately leave the 19 existing K-ID stage contracts frozen, and retain all
65 character identities.

 Exact-effect wording metric                  H4 original 56 cards                   H5 sixteen-candidate paper set
 Unique effect pairs                          22                                     37
 Cards belonging to repeated pairs            50                                     31
 Number of groups with repetitions            16                                     12
 Planned future cards rewritten as            0                                      16 of 46
 candidates
 Changes approved for live combat/stage       0                                      0
 math
The effect-pair count measures literal mechanical descriptions, NOT strategic orthogonality or win rate. A
newly distinct paragraph may still produce redundant decisions. Treat this as a narrowing of the collision
backlog, not a balance certification.

76.2 Common first-copy contract for all sixteen future variants
All Basics remain 0.85× ATK (or the previously mapped Basic if otherwise specified) with +25 personal Chakra.
Every Technique here gives its acting owner +15 Chakra, enters cooldown for the next TWO owner action
slots, and uses one normal action. Each Ultimate requires and spends 100 personal Chakra and gives none
back; one normal action. Status effects happen AFTER triggering damage unless specifically stated; none of
these cards uses a PRE-DAMAGE exception. Damage uses the unchanged DEF, Shield and modifier pipelines.

For legal targeting, a single-target rear attack still undergoes Taunt and one-hit Intercept. Row/AoE effects
ignore ordinary single-target redirect. Legal-target conditions that fail cause the published Basic fallback or
Ultimate hold. Targeting does not invent a hidden sixth shinobi, damage source, extra turn, or on-hit Chakra.

Status: PILOT. Replaces nothing until accepted in a future release-specific balance pass.

76.7 What will invalidate this research?
The proposed adjustments fail if: (1) a character with a new exact effect still has the same decisions/counters
as an existing character; (2) a visible setup cannot occur under Ultimate-first auto AI; (3) any legal five-person
composition triggers more than one normal action per unit, extra Chakra from objects or more than two
Wound sources; (4) an alternate counter is unavailable to ordinary accounts when mandatory; (5) late-Hall or
full-buff math yields an unacceptable full-health unconditional knockout; or (6) a team control/decoy/sustain
chain lacks a meaningful response window. These are OPEN checks, not passed ones.

Canon reference checkpoints for names/identity only: Naruto Official Site, Flying Raijin Jutsu (https://naruto-
official.com/en/news/01_1709); Minato column (https://naruto-official.com/en/news/01_1517); Narutopedia,
Demonic Mirroring Ice Crystals (https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Demonic_Mirroring_Ice_Crystals). Neither
source provides the RPG coefficients. Naruto commercial-use rights remain unresolved in Scope 13.

Next research H6 recommendation: Extra High. Finish the other 30 future-card uniqueness upgrades; then
conduct case-by-case five-slot authored matchups (including both orientations and failure branches),
explicitly separate theoretical rules from future empirical testing. Do not begin implementation or simulation
without user approval.

Revision record: Master 03 v1.5 | 9 Nov 2026 | H5 §76 appended only; prior content retained; companion
candidate registry and collision audit v1.5. No Scope 02/08 rules changed.

77. H6 — Thirty future-hero differentiation packets and
directed counterplay certificates
Research status: PAPER CANDIDATES / NON-AUTHORITATIVE. Hero Master v1.6; 9 November 2026. No code,
simulation, or live battle testing. The earlier 65 H3B first-copy cards and 16 H5 redesigns remain as historical
records; this section does not silently replace them.

77.1 Scope, contract hierarchy and rules that remain frozen
This pass reviews the exact thirty additional future heroes not covered by H5. The nineteen K-ID core kits
remain unchanged; no additional character is guaranteed for the introductory campaign. Primary roles are
still Tank / Damage Dealer / Support, with thirteen preserved specializations. The source of truth for direct-
damage and combat timing remains Master 02 (Sections 24, 25 and 27); the eight introductory enemy gates
remain Master 08.
 Rule / dependency                                         H6 inheritance or restriction
 Formation                                                 Five owned heroes; two front and three rear; summons/decoys
                                                           never add a sixth actor.
 Personal actions                                          One normal action per round; Ultimate first if legal with 100
                                                           Chakra; otherwise ready Technique, else Basic. An Ultimate
                                                           costs 100 Chakra, a Technique generates 15, a Basic 25.
 Technique cooldown                                        Next TWO own action slots after use. A blocked or unavailable
                                                           technique must not waste an action; ordinary Basic remains
                                                           the fallback.
 Primary statistics                                        Keep existing H3B Hall-20/Band-I HP, ATK, DEF, Speed, Crit
                                                           and Crit Damage references. No universal stat growth curve or
                                                           new hidden stat introduced.
 Statuses                                                  Mark +10% Vulnerability for next two target slots; Wound 18%
                                                           source ATK for two target slots with max two distinct sources
                                                           and a 20%-target-MaxHP raw cap per slot; Slow next-round
                                                           initiative only; Silence next target action; Stun uses shared DR
                                                           tiers.
 Caps                                                      Attack Bonus +40%; Skill Bonus +25%; Vulnerability +15%;
                                                           DEF reduction 40%; non-DEF DR 30%; Shield capacity 40%
                                                           recipient Max HP; standard status final chance 10–90%.
 Declared beneficiary lock                                 A skill that protects a lowest-HP% ally selects that ally when
                                                           the skill resolves. The Intercept or decoy does not
                                                           automatically change its protected beneficiary on a later
                                                           enemy action.
 Decoys: correction to H5                                  MASTER 02 ALREADY limits each side to two active
                                                           clones/decoys. It is not a new H6 rule and does not require
                                                           Master 02 to be changed. Third new decoy may not be created;
                                                           the original H5 proposal incorrectly described the cap as
                                                           unapproved.
 Full-health one-shot guardrail                            Ordinary non-Execute must not remove an equal-investment
                                                           full-health non-tank in normalized ranked mode. Test extreme
                                                           buff/crit/DEF stacks; values here are paper candidates.
 Passives, rarity and availability                         All numeric active passives remain unapproved; inherited
                                                           informational passives survive. No duplicate, rarity, or paid-
                                                           only requirement for essential counterplay.

77.3 Complete first-copy H6 hero amendment cards
Common Basic: the unchanged source first-copy Basic for each hero, typically 0.85× ATK on its normal legal
target, +25 personal Chakra. Each listed Technique: one normal action, +15 personal Chakra, cooldown next
two own slots. Each listed Ultimate: one normal action, costs 100 personal Chakra; no unstated extra turns,
follow-ups or Chakra. All named after-hit statuses roll once and obey the existing duration/cap rules. One-hit
DR and one-hit intercept effects never chain.

77.3.04 Killer Bee — Seven-sword rhythm / self-enhancement
 Field                                                    H6 proposed contract
 Source role and stats                                    Damage Dealer / Bruiser / Off-tank; HP 6,900 | ATK 950 | DEF
                                                          1,100 | Speed 100 | Crit 10%. Standard +25/+15/−100 Chakra
                                                          and two-own-slot Technique cooldown. No independent extra
                                                          act
 Current Basic (unchanged)                                Seven-Sword Slash — 0.85× ATK on normal legal single target;
                                                          +25 personal Chakra; no free status/clone action.
 Technique • +15 Chakra                                   Acrobat Rhythm: 0.95× ATK to legal FRONTLINE; gain +10%
                                                          Attack Bonus for the next TWO own action slots; same named
                                                          buff refreshes, not stacks.
 Ultimate • 100 Chakra                                    Eight-Tails Lariat: 1.30× ATK to legal FRONTLINE; no extra hit,
                                                          no Chakra transfer, and normal Attack Bonus bucket applies.
 Automatic selection                                      Use Technique if legal and the named Attack Bonus buff is
                                                          absent; Ultimate uses legal frontline target at 100 Chakra. If
                                                          any declared prerequisite is false, fall through to the existing
                                                          priority ladder.
 Tactical counter                                         Attack Down, Guard, and intercept can blunt the Lariat; buff
                                                          expiry denies extended synergy.
 Opportunity cost / warning                               Offensive self-buff instead of duplicating Choji’s self Shield or
                                                          the Raikage’s DR.
 Research state                                           REVISED; not live, not statistically validated

 Research state                                           RETAINED_WITH_CLARIFICATION; not live, not statistically

77.4 Readiness and effect-identity audit
 Check                                                       Result / interpretation
 Roster completeness                                         Exactly 30 H6 candidates + 16 H5 variants + 19 core K-IDs = 65
                                                             known characters. H6 candidates comprise 27 revised and 3
                                                             retained-with-clarification entries; no omissions.
 Unique H6 mechanical descriptors                            30 of 30 H6 packages have distinct annotated T/U interaction
                                                             signatures. This is an editorial categorization, NOT statistically
                                                             independent skill identities or match-winning diversity.
 Status and control                                          No new CC class, hidden resistance, evade chance, flat
                                                             penetration or true-damage multiplier. All proposed Stuns
                                                             remain DR-governed and Silence permits Basic.
 Damage bounds                                               Most pure direct single-target Ultimates in H6 are ≤1.35× ATK;
                                                             Kit designs with multi-target damage split lower coefficients
                                                             per target. Max-buff and crit tests are still required.
 AI legality                                                 Each proposal states a simple visible condition or a legal
                                                             target; a held Ultimate must fall back to ready Technique /
                                                             Basic without consuming the meter.
 H5 relationship                                             H5 remains a separate 16-character proposal set; H6 does not
                                                             quietly ratify H5 or overwrite old H3B cards.
 Future release schedule                                     Still 46 planned-release characters, NOT 46 launch
                                                             commitments. Source lore, scene art and commercial Naruto
                                                             rights are separate decisions.



77.5 Bounded directed-matchup certificates — rules, not simulated victories
Certificate means a reproducible, explicit interaction under a fixed illustrative state; it does not mean a full-
battle win certificate. No engine was executed. The reference below assumes 1,000 DEF and ordinary
noncritical damage unless otherwise stated, and abstracts unrelated actions without claiming they did not



               Scope research • Hero Roster v1.10 • Historical Sections 6, 10, 26, 69 preserved • Page 119
<PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 120 / 148>
                           NARUTO IDLE RPG | SCOPE MASTER 03 | HERO ROSTER v1.10
occur. Complete five-person schedules, all random branches, crowd-control resistance, round-by-round deaths
and win rates remain unverified.

77.5.1 MC-1 • Defensive clones versus row damage
 Certificate part                                          Explicit finding
 Five-person matchup or reference                          Decoy-focused five: Yamato / Hiruzen / Jiraiya / Konohamaru /
                                                           Shikaku. Opposing five: Temari / Naruto / Sasuke / Kakuzu /
                                                           Sakura.
 Rules and initial state                                   Master 02 Section 24.9 caps active decoys at TWO per side. A
                                                           third attempted creation must be ineligible or fail cleanly per
                                                           declared AI state; no sixth normal actor. A qualifying direct
                                                           Basic that would hit a defender may be absorbed by ONE
                                                           decoy, then that decoy disappears. An eligible rear-row AoE
                                                           from Temari still hits its declared row rather than draining
                                                           every decoy.
 Reproducible bound                                        At 1,000 ATK, 0.85× Basic versus 1,000 DEF yields
                                                           floor(1000×0.85×0.5)=425 pre-Shield. Two independent decoy
                                                           intercepts could therefore prevent at most 850 comparable
                                                           direct-HP damage in this particular equal-stat example. A
                                                           0.40× attack versus the same defense deals 200 to EACH
                                                           eligible rear target; it is not globally intercepted.
 Verdict and limitation                                    Pass: cap and targeting counterplay exist on paper. No claim
                                                           that Temari’s actual team wins, or that a third attempted
                                                           decoy automatically spends resources.



77.5.2 MC-2 • Stun and Silence sequence
 Certificate part                                          Explicit finding
 Five-person matchup or reference                          Control five: Tayuya / Kurenai / Zaku / Shisui / Itachi. Response
                                                           five: Gaara / Sakura / Naruto / Sasuke / Neji.
 Rules and initial state                                   A 60% base Stun attempt against an un-Stunned Tier-0 target
                                                           has 60% chance. After a first successful Stun consumes the
                                                           target’s next action, the target may be no longer Stunned
                                                           while its DR tier remains Tier 1: a subsequent 60%-base Stun
                                                           attempt then uses ×0.5, or 30%. If the target instead completes
                                                           a non-Stunned action without a new successful Stun, its tier
                                                           falls by one and the next attempt may return to Tier 0 (60%).
                                                           Tier-2 ×0.25 would be 15%; a repeated attempt on an
                                                           ALREADY Stunned target cannot extend lost actions or
                                                           advance tier. An 80% Silence applies separately and still
                                                           permits a Basic action (+25 Chakra).
 Reproducible bound                                        This is a conditional probability ledger, not an assertion that
                                                           any specific roll succeeded. Under the illustrative chain, a
                                                           target cannot lose two actions to two Stuns imposed before it
                                                           acts; Silence cannot cancel a Charging effect without an
                                                           explicit Interrupt tag.
 Verdict and limitation                                    Pass: repeated hard control is bounded by existing DR. Not
                                                           certified: whether this five-controller team has a fair win rate
                                                           or sufficient early-match damage.

77.5.3 MC-3 • Chakra battery ceiling
 Certificate part                                          Explicit finding
 Five-person matchup or reference                          Battery five: Inoichi / Shikaku / Karin / Cee / Naruto. Response
                                                           five: Gaara / Shikamaru / Sakura / Neji / Sasuke.
 Rules and initial state                                   Assume one recipient starts on 0 Chakra, uses a Basic (+25),
                                                           receives the MAXIMUM permitted ally-origin grant (+15) and
                                                           takes THREE qualifying incoming direct hits (+5 each, total +15
                                                           defender cap). After its first action and eligible triggers, the
                                                           illustrative upper bound is 55 Chakra, ignoring takedown
                                                           bonuses. Different batteries cannot multiply the +15 ally grant.
                                                           Technique (+15) instead of Basic (+25) would total 45.
 Reproducible bound                                        If the same maximal pattern repeats once, 55+55=110 is
                                                           clamped to 100. This does NOT prove an Ultimate can fire in
                                                           round 2: exact initiative determines whether the recipient
                                                           reaches 100 before or after its second normal action. No free
                                                           actions and no recursive Chakra generation.
 Verdict and limitation                                    Pass: bounded resource arithmetic; unresolved: actual first-
                                                           Ultimate action timing including takedown credit, opponent
                                                           target distribution and five-slot initiative.



77.5.4 MC-4 • Multi-source Wound versus triage
 Certificate part                                          Explicit finding
 Five-person matchup or reference                          Attrition five: Sasori / Konan / Kisame / Naruto / Shino.
                                                           Response five: Gaara / Chiyo / Rin / Kurenai / Sakura.
 Rules and initial state                                   Assume Sasori and Konan each previously landed one
                                                           ordinary Wound on the SAME living target; source ATK at
                                                           application = 1,000 for both, target HP Max = 6,000, DEF=1,000,
                                                           no DR/Shield. Each snapshot raw tick is 180, combined 360
                                                           versus the raw per-slot Wound cap of 1,200 (20%×6,000). The
                                                           two post-DEF ticks are 90+90=180 HP before any other
                                                           reductions.
 Reproducible bound                                        Chiyo can cleanse only ONE source and cannot retroactively
                                                           prevent a tick that happened before Chiyo’s slot. Rin’s team-
                                                           heal Ultimate is conditionally legal only if at least two living
                                                           allies are injured. Damage from Wound creates no incoming-
                                                           hit Chakra or follow-up chains.
 Verdict and limitation                                    Pass: two-source and timing accounting. Not certified: full-
                                                           round survival or whether the heal arrives before a lethal
                                                           next slot.



77.5.5 MC-5 • Setup-to-conversion and redirect
 Certificate part                                          Explicit finding
 Five-person matchup or reference                          Setup five: Gaara / Asuma / Ao / Naruto / Sakura. Response
                                                           five: Yamato / Hinata / Kakashi / Shizune / Sasuke.
 Rules and initial state                                   Ao’s proposed Mark applies AFTER Ao’s hit and so cannot
                                                           amplify that same hit. If Asuma attacks a final defender
                                                           already Marked, his proposed Ultimate is 1.35× rather than
                                                           1.25× and the standard Mark vulnerability adds +10% within
                                                           the +15% bucket. If interception changes the FINAL defender
                                                           to an unmarked body, neither the Mark-based coefficient nor
                                                           vulnerability carries over to that different body.

 Certificate part                                             Explicit finding
 Reproducible bound                                           For illustrative 1,000 ATK and 1,000 DEF, 1.25× on unmarked
                                                              defender yields floor(625). 1.35× on marked defender with
                                                              +10% vulnerability yields floor(742.5)=742. The conditional
                                                              swing is +117, NOT an invisible synergy multiplier.
 Verdict and limitation                                       Pass: legal conversion window is meaningful yet answerable.
                                                              Not certified: Asuma’s future 100-Chakra timing or whether
                                                              the target still carries Mark then.



77.5.6 MC-6 • Full-buff finisher sensitivity
 Certificate part                                             Explicit finding
 Five-person matchup or reference                             A future 1.35× non-Execute direct Ultimate against a
                                                              normalized 5,100-HP non-tank (ATK 1,250; target DEF 850).
 Rules and initial state                                      Stress assumptions: max +40% Attack Bonus, +25% Skill
                                                              Bonus, −40% effective DEF, +15% Vulnerability, critical ×2.0,
                                                              no Shield or non-DEF DR. Effective DEF = 850×0.60=510; DEF
                                                              multiplier=1000/1510.
 Reproducible bound                                           floor(1250×1.35×1.40×1.25×1000/1510×1.15×2)=4,498 HP,
                                                              leaving 602 of 5,100 HP. At 1.45× under identical conditions,
                                                              the damage is floor(4831)=4,831 and 269 HP remain. These are
                                                              conditional reference calculations, NOT evidence that every
                                                              other H6 hit is safe.
 Verdict and limitation                                       Pass for the named coefficients at this single stat reference.
                                                              Not certified against every potential character with lower HP,
                                                              alternate debuffs, or multi-hit Ultimates.

77.7 Honest closure gate and next research handoff
Completed as written research: all 30 previously unaddressed future characters have explicit first-copy
candidate ability contracts, legal-target considerations, automatic-action fallbacks, counters, and opportunity
costs. Together with H5 (16) and the preserved core K-ID set (19), this accounts for 65 named roster entries
without deleting legacy cards.

NOT completed: full 65×64 directed hero matchup matrix, fully specified ten-actor battle traces, conditional
win rates, actual rank fairness, rarity-dependent growth, final active numeric passives, or live gameplay tests.
None has been performed or inferred.

Next recommended level: Extra High. Priority H7 — full five-person matchup ledgers and frozen core-versus-
future release boundary. Use complete actor queues, every Chakra/status/Shield event, equal-investment stat
snapshots, both side priorities, and explicit unsuccessful status rolls. Then decide whether any H5/H6 proposal
becomes authoritative. Implementation stays paused until user authorization.

Canon sourcing boundary: broad signature identities are adapted from manga/anime techniques, not
represented as canon-identical mechanical outcomes. Example source leads: Inoichi Yamanaka and Mind
Body Transmission Technique entries in Narutopedia (https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Inoichi_Yamanaka;
https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mind_Body_Transmission_Technique). Character-specific lore verification
and commercial rights review remain separate requirements.

78. H7 — Complete five-versus-five paper battle traces,
conditional approval and unresolved AI risks
RESEARCH STATUS • 9 November 2026 • Master 03 v1.7. This chapter adds TWO complete conditional five-
against-five PvE battle ledgers (starter-support A/B) using a common synthetic five-enemy test formation. It is
authored arithmetic: no production combat program, randomized simulator, usability trial or win-rate study.
Earlier H2–H6 cards remain preserved and are NOT silently overwritten.

78.1 Authority, assumptions and what counts as a certificate
Authority                                                           H7 application / limitation
                                                                    Five legal slots/side; two-front/three-rear; snapshot initiative;
                                                                    seeded tie token alternates per round; one normal action/round;
Master 02 §§24.2–24.9; §27.5
                                                                    target selector → redirect → damage/Shield → HP → Chakra →
                                                                    durations.
                                                                    Basic +25; Technique +15; Ultimate spends 100; defender +5 per
                                                                    distinct eligible direct root attack capped +15 per round; root-
Chakra and skill clock
                                                                    action takedown +10; Technique unavailable for the next TWO
                                                                    owner slots.
                                                                    No crits or Attack/Skill bonuses in the main trace; floor(ATK × coeff
Damage formula                                                      × max[0.35,1000/(1000+target DEF)]). Wound 18% of caster ATK
                                                                    snapshot, DEF mitigation, no direct-hit Chakra.
                                                                    Use Gaara, Naruto, Sasuke, Neji, Sakura/Shizune as written; Neji
H2 first-copy cards / status outcomes                               Mark 80% roll is explicitly FAILED in the main trace; enemy
                                                                    Wound 80% roll SUCCEEDS; all ordinary direct attacks noncritical.

78.8 Findings, contradictions and next exact research handoff
Findings: (1) Sakura and Shizune have different, legal first-copy responses to a Wound source; both
conditional branches clear the five-enemy harness in round 5. (2) Gaara Taunt redirects one declared Sasuke-
targeted Raider hit in round 2; AoE bypass and future untelegraphed cases remain separate. (3) A defensive
shield can change Sasuke’s Execute legality while leaving the team’s round of victory unchanged. (4) A healer
may present 780 gross healing yet restore only 531. (5) Wound persists after its applier is knocked out; a
Cleanse preceding the victim slot removes future ticks but never reverses past damage. (6) An Ultimate’s
action priority can waste a legal shield during a fight already about to end; we must not hide that limitation.

Unresolved AI contracts: How does Sand Guard know an eligible future single-target threat in an
untelegraphed Arena? Does Triage make a legal safe choice from public information only? These are
specification gaps, not excuses to silently grant omniscient AI. Keep this module UNAPPROVED for competitive
release until deterministic preview inputs are defined in Master 02 and reconciled with Master 03.

Next: H7B should calculate one equal-investment PvP two-leg mirror from beginning to resolution using
explicit public intent inputs; H8 should examine counterplay across multi-decoy, stun/Silence and Chakra
acceleration squads. No production code or executable game engine is authorized by this research.


79. H7B — Mirrored Arena ordering and public-information
defensive AI
RESEARCH STATUS • 9 November 2026 • Hero Master 03 v1.8. This is desk-based, conditional paper research,
not a runnable combat simulator, matchmaker or verified ranked win rate. Master 02 §§24–27, Master 10 §61,
Master 03 §70/§78 remain authoritative. No earlier hero cards, campaign gates, Arena decision, or combat
arithmetic are rewritten in this section.

79.1 Why PvE previews cannot silently become ranked omniscience
§78 identified the missing Arena threat preview. H2 Gaara Sand Guard and Shizune Medical Ward were
conditioned on previewed future single-target intent. In scripted PvE, designers can openly declare an enemy
charge or targeted action. In live ranked with both squads locked before reveal, allowing an AI to see the
other side’s private future selected action is unjustified. A published skill/Speed/Chakra snapshot is public
state; a prediction of a specific uncommitted future action is not.

Gaara candidate, independent from Shizune: permit Sand Guard only when a living OTHER ally is ≤75% HP
and an opponent currently has a legal single-target path to that ally based on public kit range, or when an
already visibly committed eligible attack exists. The 75% threshold is only an illustrative first-copy option; this
is not inserted into §70. Self-only pressure should not waste a Taunt that protects nobody. No defensive tool
may preview a hidden future status or opponent target.

79.3 Full four-round side-priority stress test — synthetic, ineligible units
Purpose: verify ordering and the two-leg draw scoring at the simplest five-versus-five boundary. This is
deliberately NOT an Arena-eligible Naruto roster or proof of 19-hero balance. Both sides use five identical
dummy units with HP850, ATK1000, DEF1000, Speed100; each has only a Basic 0.85× (425 noncritical HP
damage), no Technique/Ultimate/status/Shield, and visible default front-first targeting. An actual ranked unit
has more actions and cannot be replaced by this fixture. Side priority groups exact-Speed units by side and
alternates each round; defeated queued slots are skipped.

Leg 1 uses Left=A, Right=B: A wins at round 4 with only A rear-right at 850 HP. Leg 2 swaps owner placement
to Left=B, Right=A while retaining the identical publicly documented slot harness: B wins with only B rear-
right at 850 HP. Two-leg points: A=1, B=1; match is a draw, with no invented HP or speed tiebreak (§61). This
proves that the stipulated simple ordering can be traced and side swapping changes the beneficiary. It does
not prove general side fairness, since other abilities, RNG and uneven Speed may produce unequal outcomes.

79.4 Two-round authentic hero opening — strict first-copy reading
Actual roster comparison (not a complete match): Team A = Gaara FL, Naruto FR, Sasuke RL, Neji RM, Sakura

79.7 Conditional approval register, rejected shortcuts and H7C handoff
RETAIN, without modifying original kit values: nine H2 cards and both H7 starter benchmark ledgers; 19
common ranked slots stay theoretical until full skill/behavior approval. PROMOTE to documentation-only
conclusion: ranked AI must be public-information-safe, and mirroring alone does not prove balanced team
outcomes. DO NOT PROMOTE Shizune’s ≤75%-HP Ward condition, Gaara defensive alteration, event-keyed
seed structure, a new team HP% formula, or any future H5/H6 hero directly into authoritative combat. All
require cross-scope review. Rejected: pretending Gaara can see an uncommitted Sasuke-targeted attack;
treating synthetics as real ranked characters; reporting two-round HP as a match winner; ignoring cooldown
and Chakra; inventing new tie-breakers.

NEXT: H7C priority — complete real-Naruto ten-round/KO paired-leg 5v5 certificates under a deliberately
chosen approved public-information policy (or find and record a contradiction); settle the exact ten-round
team-HP% aggregation and event-keyed RNG policy with Combat Master 02/Arena Master 10; then extend
ledgers to stun, silence, decoys and Chakra batteries. Continue theory only; no executable game or seed Monte
Carlo until expressly authorized. Recommended thinking: Extra High.

80. H7C — Full mirrored PvP paper ledgers: real starter shinobi,
fixed adverse Mark branch
RESEARCH STATUS • 9 November 2026 • Master 03 v1.9. Authoring and arithmetic-validation only. No
executable combat simulator, matchmaker, live game, or statistically meaningful battle sample. All earlier H2–
H7B records remain intact. Master 02 §27.5 and Master 10 §61 remain source of truth; this appendix makes no
combat-rule or roster-eligibility changes.

80.1 Exact authority, permitted state and adverse-branch meaning
This paper fixture extends the already published H7B starter mirror through all ten ranked rounds using only
nine previously specified Hall-20/Band-I hero contracts (§70). It does NOT apply H7B candidate Policy B
(Shizune Medical Ward at ≤75% public HP) or Gaara’s proposed public-health Taunt; those remain
unapproved. Here, no enemy publicly commits an eligible future hostile attack; Gaara and Shizune therefore
never activate forecast-dependent Sand Guard / Medical Ward. Their existing HP-conditional Ultimates are
legal when their own thresholds occur.

Teams and positions: A = Gaara (FL), Naruto (FR), Sasuke (RL), Neji (RM), Sakura (RR); B replaces Sakura with
Shizune at RR. Gaara HP7800/ATK800/DEF1200/SPD90; Naruto 6000/1000/1000/100; Sasuke 5100/1250/850/110;
Neji 5700/850/950/115; Sakura/Shizune 6300/800/1000/95. Each begins at 0 Chakra. Both sides start with 30,900
total Max HP.

Fixed, specifically adverse branch: EACH Neji 80%-chance Mark roll in rounds 1, 4 and 7 FAILS. All attacks are
noncritical. No Wound, Charging, debuff, buffs, clones, crit, equipment mismatch, or hidden future-action
preview occurs. This low-probability branch is intentionally not representative of expected ranked win rates;
the purpose is to stress action legality, cooldowns, changing HP targets, support timing and side-priority
rather than measure general strength.

Actor queue: Speed descending, then alternating exact-Speed side priority, then slot. Round 1 favors LEFT,
round 2 RIGHT, and so on. Leg 1 places A LEFT/B RIGHT. Leg 2 places B LEFT/A RIGHT. Frontline-lowest-HP%
target selection is public. When both fronts are defeated, the lowest-HP%-rear target is attacked. A defeated
queued actor is logged as a skip; a unit defeated in an earlier round is not requeued.

80.5 Two-leg reconciliation, round cap and no fabricated win rate
 Fixture                           A current HP (of 30,900)           B current HP (of 30,900)       Conditional cap outcome
 Leg 1 • A LEFT/B RIGHT            14,692 = 47.5469%                  15,769 = 51.0324%              B wins on higher HP%
 Leg 2 • B LEFT/A RIGHT            14,438 = 46.7249%                  16,602 = 53.7282%              B wins on higher HP%
                                                                                                     B wins THIS explicit adverse
 Two-leg score                     A 0 points                         B 2 points                     paper branch; no rating
                                                                                                     predicted
Shield is excluded from HP% numerator. For this same-MaxHP pair, using the sum of surviving current HP
divided by the original sum of five MaxHP values gives the recorded numbers. Master 02 states HP%
remaining but has not published an exact cross-roster denominator convention. This fixture must not silently
establish the general rule for dissimilar rosters; carry that as a technical specification item for Master 02 and
Master 10 review.

Round 4 is a particularly important correction to an over-simple mirror assumption. Both Gaaras drop to 51
HP after the support Basics (they do not both die). The round-4 priority Gaara acts first and knocks out the
opposing Gaara; that survivor is killed by the other Neji at round-5 opening. This difference determines
whether Sakura or Shizune meets the ≤80% Ultimate threshold in R5. Also, at R8 Sasuke’s ≤30%-HP Execute
checks must occur at the precise action slot: in Leg 1 it KOs A Naruto; in Leg 2 it leaves A Naruto at 251 HP,
allowing B Naruto to make the eventual knockout. A generic round-total damage shortcut would be incorrect.

Validity boundaries: each per-actor hit is checked against the existing DEF floor formula; queue, target,
Chakra, cooldown and Ultimate conditions are independently reconciled against the authored steps. The
branch intentionally forces six Neji Mark failures per leg despite each individual Mark having an 80% success
chance, so the shown 2–0 is neither an expected result nor evidence that Shizune is stronger on average.
Untraced successful Mark/critical outcomes could change action deaths, shield timing and winner; no
frequencies are assigned.

80.7 Next narrow research ticket — H7D
Repeat this same first-copy two-leg matchup with an explicitly documented successful-Mark outcome and
separate critical/noncritical branches. Track every status application after hit, target-slot expiry, and legal
Gaara/Support AI state. Then compare whether a squad containing Shikamaru or Shino changes support
substitution and counterplay. Only after several genuinely different tactical branches should numeric passive
permissions, public-health defensive Technique thresholds or roster-wide balance approval be reconsidered.
Continue theory first: do not implement a runnable combat simulator or start game code without user
instruction.


81. H7D: Mark, Crit and Wound sensitivity - conditional first-
divergence paper audit
RESEARCH STATUS | 9 November 2026 | Hero Master v1.10. This chapter preserves all prior H2–H7C
statements as history. It is a reproducible branch sensitivity study, not a simulated win-rate report or an
approved rebalance. It does not replace the two H7C full-match ledgers and does not change Combat Master
02 or Arena Master 10.

81.1 Source authority, test fixture and what counts as a certificate
Source priority: Combat Scope 02 §§24.2–24.7 and 25.8–25.10; Hero Scope 03 §70.3 current core kits; H7C §80
ten-round adverse-branch receipts; Arena Scope 10 §61 two-leg scoring. H7C deliberately forced all six Neji
80%-chance Mark attempts per leg to fail and all direct attacks to be noncritical. The present study changes
only an explicitly named branch variable; all other state is inherited up to its FIRST resulting action/target
divergence. A changed target, knockout, shield, HP predicate, Chakra-gain source or Ultimate means no later
H7C value can be reused without a fresh complete ledger.

81.2 Why the all-fail branch cannot support a ranking
If all six Neji Mark rolls truly occur and are independent with p(success)=0.80, the probability of exactly k
successes follows Binomial(n=6,p=0.8). Zero successful Marks has probability (0.2)^6=0.000064 = 0.0064%, or
about 1 in 15,625 such six-attempt sequences. Under independently drawn two-leg sequences, all twelve
failing is (0.2)^12=0.000000004096, approximately 1 in 244,140,625. These are probabilities of declared roll
patterns, not probabilities of losing, and the H7C fight can change the number of future attempts once earlier
successes change the queue.

 Successful Marks among 6              Pattern probability                  Percent

 0                                     0.000064000                          0.00640%

 1                                     0.001536000                          0.15360%

 2                                     0.015360000                          1.53600%
 3                                     0.081920000                          8.19200%

 4                                     0.245760000                          24.57600%

 5                                     0.393216000                          39.32160%

 6                                     0.262144000                          26.21440%


81.3 Exact Mark-hit receipts: no free damage on the applying hit
Neji Chakra Point Survey is 0.55× ATK, landing before the separate Mark roll. A successful Mark grants +10%
Vulnerability on subsequent legal hits for the next two action slots belonging to that target. There is no
retroactive boost to Neji’s applying hit. With Gaara DEF=1,200, unbuffed ATK and no crit, each direct attack
uses floor(ATK × coefficient × 1000/(1000+1200) × [1.10 if Mark active else 1.00]), flooring once per hit.
