The first Technique applies Mark only after dealing 212. At R1, a successful Neji Mark makes the subsequent
Sasuke, Naruto, support and Gaara attacks deal 875, 650, 340 and 340 instead of 795, 590, 309 and 309. This
changes the R1 Gaara end HP from 5,585 to 5,383 on BOTH sides. The mark persists through that Gaara’s first
and second target action slots; the precise R2 side-order breaks symmetry by a single Basic damage difference
of 31.

81.4 Both mirrored legs: fully bounded prefix through first R4 knockout
Named conditional branch M++: on each leg, BOTH Neji R1 and BOTH Neji R4 Mark rolls succeed; all ordinary
damage remains noncritical. R1–R3 have the same legal targets as H7C. In R2, the Gaara acting first clears its
second-slot Mark before the opposite Gaara Basic, so its HP loss in that round is 1,966 rather than 1,997. R3 has
no active Mark and retains the former 1,814 per-Gaara damage. At R4, each Neji Technique re-applies Mark;
each Sasuke Technique deals 875 and each Naruto Technique 650. Both Gaaras are now knocked out in
Naruto’s slots 5–6, BEFORE the support and Gaara slots. The audit STOPS at this first target/action divergence;
R4 support and later rounds need new target and Chakra ledgers.

81.5 Critical-hit sensitivity - not a made-up Crit Chance
Separately hold all Marks failed as in H7C. In R4 the victim Gaara has 1,745 HP just after Neji’s 212 Technique.
Sasuke’s 1.40× Chidori against 1,200 DEF deals 795 noncrit, or 1,193 with an illustrative Crit Damage factor
1.50, or 1,590 with factor 2.00 (the current cap). The subsequent Naruto Rasengan deals 590 unless a changed
target/KO prevents it. The core Hall20 passport does not publish Sasuke’s final Crit Damage and Crit Chance in
this first-copy stat table, so 1.50× and 2.00× are SENSITIVITY VALUES, not asserted live probabilities.

 R4 Sasuke              Sasuke hit             Gaara HP after         Gaara HP after         Decision impact
 outcome                                       Sasuke                 Naruto 590

                                                                                             Gaara survives into
 Noncrit 1.00x          795                    950                    360
                                                                                             support slots

 Illustrative Crit                                                                           Gaara dies during
                        1193                   552                    0
 1.50x                                                                                       Naruto slot

 Max allowed Crit                                                                            Gaara dies during
                        1590                   155                    0
 2.00x                                                                                       Naruto slot

A single early critical hit can change target legality, whether the later Gaara attacks, the recipient of support
healing, defender on-hit Chakra, and the match cap result. Do NOT take the 1,077/2,164 HP differential from
H7C and subtract the Crit increment to claim an outcome. The next complete-branch ledger must specify
explicit Crit Chance, Crit Damage, and the roll sequence or predeclared seed.

81.6 Wound pressure, cleansing and shield: separate controlled fixture
Status fixture W2 is NOT appended to the PvP mirror. Two independent Wound appliers each have ATK after
Attack Down 900; target Naruto has DEF 1,000 and Max HP 6,000; non-DEF reduction is 0, no other direct hits,
Healing Suppression or shields at tick start. Each source snapshots 900×18%=162 pre-DEF and deals
floor(162×1,000/(1,000+1,000))=81 HP per recipient action slot, for up to two slots. Combined two-source
damage is 162 per slot and 324 over two slots, below the 20%-MaxHP-per-slot cap (1,200). This is NOT a
statement that source ATK 900 is a canonical enemy unit or that Sakura and Shizune have equal first-turn
activation.

Neither support is inherently better for all Wound states: Sakura permanently removes one source and can
save later shield budget; Shizune can prevent several types of damage within her shield duration, but relies
on an eligible threat preview for her Technique. Each decision depends on action order, incoming hits, source
targeting and actual AI trigger. A scripted PvE threat could satisfy that trigger; an unannounced ranked
opponent action generally does not under the currently frozen first-copy policy.

Negative evidence matters: substituting Shikamaru does not beat a team with no Charging skills, and
substituting Shino does not bypass the two-front/three-rear target restrictions. Both specialists are useful in
their intended contexts, but their counter labels cannot be treated as guaranteed solutions to every support or
sustained-damage matchup. The relevant story characters remain deterministic acquisitions, not compulsory
limited gacha units.
